7, 16 and 2th stars are governed by Jupiter. 


8, 17 and 26! stars are governed by Saturn. 
9, 18 and 27th stars are governed by Mercury. 


Kethu is the significator of the houses 8,10,5,7 and 4. Hence 
whichever be the planet that transits the Constellations governed by 
Kethu. i.e. whichever planet passes through the Stars 1,10 and 19. 
i.e. O° to 13°-20°; 120° to 133°-20'; 240° to 253°-20', these transiting 
planets offer the matters signified by the houses 8,10,5,7 and 4. 
Kethu, the significator of 8,10,5,7 and 4 offers these matters during 
the occasion whenever planets transit in any of its three stars; the 
Source is indicated by the planet transiting. 


Suppose 8th house indicates money of the partner; 7th denotes 
partner and 4th house business of partner, then 8,7 and 4 indicate 
the above matters. If significator of 5 transits, partner gains through 
music or speculation or through children. If the 12th house significator 
transits, partner makes money by service or by borrowing, making 
money by husband or wife or partner is shown by Kethu being the 
significator of the houses 8, 7 and 4. How the money is made, is 
indicated by the, houses signified by the transiting planet. 


Next take 13°-20’ to 26°40"; 133° to 146°-40'; 253°-20' to 266°- 
40". 


They are called Bharani, Poorvapalguni and Poorvashada Stars. 
These are ruled by Venus. Venus is the significator of the houses 4, 
11, 7 and 8. Hence any planet transiting in these constellations will 
bring out such matters of the houses 4,11,7 and 8. If in a housewife's 
chart, the above transit is found, it means, to her husband it shows 
1,2 10 and 5 houses. So the husband may start Brokerage and 
jobbing business in Stock Exchange; or open a Book Shop in Race 
Club at the time when the 12th house significator of the housewife i.e. 
6th house significator of the husband transits. 


157 


Suppose significator of the 6th from 7th enter into the 
constellation belonging to Venus, do not expect the result on the day 
of entry. You have to wait till the significator of the 6th comes to the 
sub belonging to the significator of 11. If the significator of 6th house is 
Jupiter and it has entered in Poorvapalguni (133°-20'to 146°-40') 
constellation, till it touches Venus Sub and Mercury Sub-sub or 
Mercury Sub in Venus Star, one cannot be promoted, even though 
both Venus and Mercury are the significators of 11 and Jupiter, the 
transiting planet is the significator of 6. Mercury Sub in Venus Star 
commences at 144° and extends up to 145"-53'-20". 


Star 3,12 and 21 are ruled by Sun. They are called Karthik, 
Uthrapalguni and Uthrashada stars. They extend from 26°- 40' to 40°; 
146-40' to 160°; 266°40' to 280°. Sun is the significator of the houses , 
5 and 7. Whenever any planet transits, it shows love affairs, romance, 
payment of share or dividend to the partner. Love affairs will be 
pleasant when the significator of 7 transits the sub of 11th house 
significator. But when the same planet transit in the Sub of the 
significator of 6th house, the girl may go away and there will be 
separation till it comes to the sub governed by Significator of 11. 


Saturn is the significator of 7. It moves slowly. So the duration 
will be long when Saturn transits. Sun is also the significator of 


7.So when Sun transits in the same constellation and sub the 
duration of separation will be short 


Kethu and Jupiter transiting cause separation for a longer 
period. 


Stars 4, 13 and 22 are governed by Moon. They are called 
Rohini, Hastham and Sravanam. Moon is the significator of 4,1,5,6 
and 9. If the significator of 5th house transits either 40° to 53°-20' or 
160°to 173°-20' or 280° to 293°-20' one will go on leave, make long 
journey, reaches his native place and remains there.

Whenever Kethu transits whichever matter is signified by the 
lord of the Star, Kethu offers through the matters signified by it. 
Kethu is the significator of 7th house. So, through partner or 
opponent (indicated by 7th house), the matter indicated by the lord of 
the constellation comes through. But it cannot come through 11th 
house matters whereas the same position transited by Venus or 
Mercury can give. 


Whenever Sun transits, the matters signified by the lord of the 
constellation will come through, through the matters signified by Sun 
i.e. 20r5or 7. 


If Moon transits a particular constellation, whatever the lord 
of the constellation signifies will be given by Moon during its transit 
through the matters of the houses signified by Moon i.e. houses 4, 
1,5, 6 and 9. 


If Mars transits a constellation, whatever the lord of the 
constellation, signifies will be offered by Mars through the matters 
signified by Mars i.e. 4, 8, 7, 5 and 10. 


If the lord of the constellation indicates ‘income’ or receipt of 
money if the significator of 3rd houses transits, the income will be 
by M.O. or cheque; from brother or neighbour; from editing or 
publishing, by Brokerage and so on. 


If the significator of 4th house transits, one gets as rent for 
the house let by him or one gets the yield or from mother. 


If the significator of 5th house transits, one can have the 


income through music, opera, cinema or speculation or through 
children.

Thus the source is indicated by the transiting planet and what will 
happen is shown by the lord of the constellation, whether it will 
materialise or not, whether it is favourable or not, is indicated by the 
sublord according to the houses signified by the sublord. 


DASA BHUKTHI ANTHRA AND TRANSIT- 





There are 27 stars. Planets taken by Hindus are 9. As 
mentioned already each governs 3 stars. Depending on the portion 
to be covered, in a star by Moon and the lord of the constellation, the 
balance of the dasa ruled by the lord of the constellation is 
calculated. Then, in cyclic order the other dasas follow. For detailed, 
clear and exhaustive explanation, kindly refer to the book ‘1st 
Reader-Casting the horoscope of "KRISHNAMURTI PADHDHATI”. 


As there are 9 planets, there are 9 dasas. Each dasa has 
nine Bhukties; Hence one may expect there would be 81 
combinations. But actually the combination will be 45. 


When we take anthras, there should be 9 X 9 X 9 = 729. But the 
same combination i.e. ‘A’ dasa ‘B’ Bhukthi 'C Anthra; 'A’ dasa ‘C’ 
Bhukthi ‘B’Anthra; 'B' dasa 'A' Bhukthi ‘C’Anthra; 'B' dasa 'C Bhukthi 
‘A' Anthra; 'C dasa 'B' Bhukthi 'A’ Anthra ‘C Dasa 'A' Bhukthi ‘B‘ 
Anthra are the combinations of the three planets A, B, and C in 6 
different conjoined periods of these three. Again Dasa lord itself may 
be bhukthi lord and anthra lord. They are 9 in number instead of 18. 
Then Dasa lord, another planets' Bhukthi and Dasa lord anthra or 
Bhukthi lord anthra. Thus you can have permutation and combination. 


In the map of Heavens erected according to Krishnamurti 
padhdhati there are 249 subs and so 249 combinations. Of these 
certain positions in the Zodiac are ruled by the same group of 


For example we have, 


161 


Mars Sign; Venus Star, Venus Sub 13°-20'to I5°-33'-20" 

Venus Sign, Mars Star, Venus Sub I82MO'to 184°-53'-20" 

These two points are ruled by Mars and Venus. 

Mars Sign, Venus star, Sun Sub 15°-33'-20"to 16°-13'-20" 

Sun sign, Venus Star, Mars Sub 137°-20'to 138"-6'A0'. 

Venus Sign, MarsStar, Sun Sub 184=-53'-20"to 185°-33'.20". 
These three points are governed by the same group of planets. 
Venus Sign, Jupiter Star, Moon Sub 209°-26'-40* to 210°, Jupiter 
Sign, Venus Star, Moon Sub 256M3'20" to 257°-20" only two. 


Saturn Sign, Mars Star, Mercury Sub 300° to 301°-53'-20" in 
Kumba; Mars Sign, Saturn Star, Mercury Sub 215°-26'-40" to 
217°-20' - Vrishika; Mars Sign, Mercury Star, Saturn Sub 
237°-53'20" to 240° Vrishika; Mercury Sign, Mars Star, Saturn 
Sub 177°-53'-20" to 180°. Thus there are four different points 
for the group of Mars, Saturn and Mercury. 


Therefore when A dasa B Bhukthi C Anthra operates, that 
part of the Zodiac governed by these three will be transited by 
them, or luminaries and Lagna. For example if it is Venus dasa, 
Mars Bhukthi, Venus anthra, then the position to be transited will 
be Mars, Venus, Venus, or Venus, Mars, Venus. If Mars dasa, 
Kethu Bhukthi, Kethu anthra operates, then 0° to 0°46'40" may be 
transited; or Kethu Star, Mars Sub, Kethu sub-sub or Kethu Star, 
Kethu Sub, Mars Sub-sub will be transited by Mars, Kethu; or Sun, 
Moon and on that day, the moment when the ascendant which 
rises in the East for the locality in which the native, then, is so that 
the sign lord, star lord, and sub lord agree. One should not always 
calculate the ascendant for the place of birth unless the native is 
permanent in his birth place. 


162 


In the case of Rahu and Kethu, the planet with which they 
are conjoined, or the lord of the constellation in which Rahu and 
Kethu were at the time of birth or the lord of the sign can give the 
result in their Star or Sub: also the lord of the day will be ruled by 
any one of the above in the order given. Suppose Rahu is in 
Aquarius conjoined with Jupiter; and in Dhanishta Star. Then 
Thursday ruled by Jupiter; or if no conjunction Mars by Tuesday. If 
Saturn is stronger than Mars, then Saturn can give the result on 
Saturday. Whenever Rahu rules the dasa or Bhukthi; the matter 
will materialise during Rahu-Kalam followed in Tamil Nadu. This 
also will agree. 


Table for 249 subs is furnished below, Lord of the sign, Star 
and Sub are also given. If one runs Venus dasa, Moon Bhukthi, 
Mercury anthra, refer Venus Sign, Moon star, Mercury Sub: Moon 
Sign, Mercury Star, Venus sub: Mercury Sign, Moon Star, Venus 
sub. 


So also if one runs the conjoined period of Merc., Rahu, 
Jupiter, refer Mercury sign, Rahu star, Jupiter sub; note also 
Jupiter sign, Mercury star, Rahu Sub. If one runs conjoined period 
of Sun, Venus, Mercury,then Sun sign, Venus star, Mercury Sub, 
Venus sign, Sun star, Mercury sub, and Mercury sign, Sun Star, 
Venus sub are the three sensitive and fruitful positions which will 
be transited by Ascendant, Luminaries and the planets ruling the 
dasa, bhukthi and anthra.

TRANSIT RESULTS 
(LECTURE AT CALCUTTA) 


Today the subject chosen isTransit and its Results. This is one 
of the methods to find out what a person can expect due to the 
movement of the planets in various signs as days pass on. General 
predictions offered in dailies, weeklies and monthlies are based on 
either of the two systems Hindu orWestern. 


Hindus take the position of Moon in Nirayana chart at the time 
of the birth of a person whereas Westerners take the position of 
Sun at one's birth time. Hindus find out in which sign a planet 
transits and how many signs away it is, when counted from the 


171 


Sign occupied by Moon. They do not take into consideration whether 
Moon is in the 1st degree or in the middle or in the end of the sign; so 
also a planet transiting in a sign may be in the beginning or in the 
middle or in the end. The actual longitudinal distance is never 
calculated and included for prediction. Suppose Moon is in the first 
degree of the sign Mesha and if a planet is in the beginning or in the 
middle or in the end in Rishaba it is always said to be in the 2nd sign 
to Moon sign and hence in 2 to Moon. 


The longitudinal distance will range between 30° and 60°. If 
Moon were to be over 29° in Aries and a planet just leaves Aries and 
enters into Taurus it is said to be in the second sign till it leaves 
Taurus. Therefore the longitudinal distance between the two will be 1° 
to 30°. Does it sound alright to call a planet in 2 to Moon when the 
longitudinal distance varies between 1° and 60°. When the distance is 
only 1° are they not in conjunction? Only in the map we draw for a 
horoscope, we draw lines to separate each sign. In the heavens is 
there any partition wall? Will not both the planets appear very close to 
each other? If the declination is the same then Moon alone can be 
seen and the planet will be hidden. When actually a planet is in rapt 
conjunction to say that it is in the second house to Moon and also to 
call a planet in 29° Aries, when Moon is in the first degree of Aries, as 
conjoined with Moon in Janma Rasi, does not appeal alright. 


Secondly, if a person has a horoscope let him take it. Note the 
lagna. On that day there were some births. Suppose Moon on that 
day was in Cancer. Then, think awhile. Does the position of Moon to 
all the births on that day denote the same result to all. Or whom does 
Moon indicate? For Aries lagna born, Moon is lord of 4 in 4; So it 
denotes mother; to Taurus - born it indicates younger brother and 
sister, to Cancer lagna born, the person himself; to Virgo born, elder 
brothers, sisters, friends, younger paternal uncle, son-in-law, 
daughter-in-law etc., to Scorpio born, Moon denotes father; to 
Capricorn borns Moon indicates wife; to Pisces borns, children, etc., 
When such is the case, if the 


172 


position of transiting planets is favourable will the person enjoy or 
such a relative as is indicated by Moon according to one's lagna 
enjoy? which appeals to us? Which comes correct. Therefore how to 
judge, is to be solved. 


Sun is taken by Westerners. Similarly judge whom Sun 
indicates. Suppose Sun is in Leo. to Aries borns Sun indicates 
children; to Taurus - borns mother; To Gemini - borns younger 
brother, etc., Therefore good aspects to Sun will bestow desirable 
results to such a relative as is shown by Sun by occupation and 
ownership and predictions offered taking the position of sun, 
excluding lagna is also not correct. 


But when one has no horoscope, then to offer some mental 
solace, there should be some base, some method. So Hindus take 
Moon; Westerners take Sun, That is why the general predictions 
which are published taking either of the luminaries have to fail and 
they fail in majority cases. Not knowing the truth one may attribute 
the difference to one's will. As lagna indicates one's health, longevity 
success or failure etc., one must take only the lagna, then the 
position of all planets and the |2 cusps. 


Requisites: Place of birth, actual time of birth, date etc., 
Krishman's Ephemeris for the year of birth and Table of Houses. 
Erect the chart using these. Deduct Krishnamurti Ayanamsa for that 
year. Mark all the twelve cusps; also all the planets and fortuna. 


Next write down First house; Second House; Third 
house and so on till Twelfth house, one below the other. 


Always take that a house extends from that cusp to the next cusp 
(exact degree and minute). Do not mix up - Note down in which house 
each planet is. 


Then, re-assort (re-classify). Note in which house 
Kethu was atthe time of birth; which matters will be signified by 
that house; also 


173 


consider which other house Kethu can _ represent; 
include the matters of that house also. 


Kethu Governs Aswini, Makam and Moolam. Therefore in the 
areas 0° to 13°20', 120° to 133°-20' and 240° to 253°20' note down 
the matters signified by the house which Kethu is to represent. 


Take Venus. Note down which house it occupies and also which 
cusps are found in Taurus and Libra so that Venus becomes the lord 
of those houses. Hence the matters denoted by the houses occupied 
and owned by Venus will be the nature of result which the 2nd, 11th 
and 20th stars i.e. 13°20' to 26°40’; 133°20'to 146°40° and 253°20' to 
266°40' indicates 


Sun governs Karthik, Uthrapalguni and Uthrashada, the 3rd, 12th 
and 21st stars. Whatever sun denotes by occupation and ownership 
will be the results indicated by these stars, similarly note for all 
planets including Rahu and Kethu who rule the stars 6, 15, and 24 
and 1, 10 and 19 respectively. 


Thus you will find that all the 360° of the zodiac, divided into 27 
constellation; denote 9 groups of results, each repeating thrice. 


Whenever a planet transits, it will be in any of the 27 
constellations. Whatever the lord of the constellation, according to its 
occupation and ownership denote, will be given by the planet 
transiting in any of the three constellations ruled by that planet. The 
source is shown by the transiting planet according to its nature, 
occupation and ownership. 


Suppose there are 27 rooms with nine different varieties of 
provisions. 


Those varieties which is stored in room 1, is also 
stored in room 10 and room 19 ; say paddy - rice. 


Suppose wheat is stored in room 2, it is also found in 
room 11 and 20. 


174 


If sugar is stored in room 39 it is also stocked in room 12 and 24. 
Kerosene if stocked in room 4, it is also kept in room 13 and 22. 


Also suppose there are 9 salesman. That salesman in room 1 
or 10 or 19 can give only rice or paddy; in room 2 or 11 or 20 can 
give only wheat; in room 3 or 12 or 21 can give only sugar; in 
room 4 or 130r 22 can supply kerosene and so on. If there are 
2 or 3 people in the same room, all will give me same, so, the 
quality and the nature of the provision which is to be distributed 
remains the same for each room; salesmen may be A or B orC; 
Sun or Moon or Mars or Mercury and so on. But yet what they 
can give depends on the material stored in that room, "Who 
supplies", if that question arises, then the stuff is given by that 
person who is in that room. Therefore how one gets; through 
whom one gets i.e., the source is shown by the person in the 
room i.e., the planet transiting. The planet indicates the source by 
its occupation and ownership of houses. 


Suppose a planet, say Sun, is in Uthraphalguni 1st quarter 
at 27° Leo and it is in the 11th house to Libra born. Sun is lord of 
11; itis in 11. It is in the constellation of lord of 11. Hence it 
promises profit; success; realisation of ambition, etc., Sun 
governs Karthik, Uthraphalguni and Uthrashada. The 
constellation may be in 7 or 8 or 11 or 12 or 3 or 4th Bhava: In 
whichever Bhava it is, planets transiting in any one of these three 
stars must produce most advantageous and agreeable results. 


But suppose Jupiter transits in any of these 3 stars. Jupiter 
is in 3. Then Jupiter as lord of 3 in 3 and as lord 6 shows that 
through the matters indicated by the houses 3 and 6, he gains; 
through younger brother; by making shortjourney; by editing, 
publicity etc., or through maternal uncle etc., he may gain. It is 
also indicated that he may recover from the disease, feel healthy 
and make friendship with one who attended on him. 6th house, 
disease, that is the cause: 3rd house acquaintance and both 3 
and 6 show assistants and attendants. 11th house indicates 
friendship. 


175 


Therefore it must be clearly understood that every point in the 
Zodiac is governed by a planet, depending on the constellation in 
which that point has fallen. 


Next, one should note the beneficial and adverse planets to 
oneself. By beneficial it is meant that planet which can fulfill his 
desire. Suppose one wants to go overseas, then the significators of 
the houses 3, 9 and 12 are beneficial; suppose one wants to commit 
suicide, then the significator of the 8th house is beneficial to him then. 
Suppose one wants scholarship for studies then significators of the 
houses 4, 6, 9 and 11 are good. 


If one wants to marry, significators of 2, 7 and 11; if he wants a 
divorce then the significators of the houses 6, 12, 10. 8 will finalise the 
divorce. 


Next we have to remember that when the constellation is ruled 
by the significator of the second house and if it is the ruling planet of 
the partner in business, the native gains money but the partner faces 
difficulties as the second house is the 8th to the 7th. But if the 
significator of the 2nd house is the ruling planet of wife, she 
experiences difficulties, in spite of husband's gains. Children get 
promoted as the second house is the 10th to the 5th; mother makes 
good profit as it is the 11th house to the 4th; younger brother loses, or 
changes job or he is hospitalised or cheated, father falls ill and incurs 
debt and so on. Thus it can be seen that the person may gain, earn 
etc., whereas different results will be enjoyed by different relatives. If a 
pin pricks in any part of your body, it pains in that area you feel it. 
Similarly close kith and kin, if they suffer, one will be worried, though 
personally one can have some small fortune in one's efforts. 
Prediction will be incomplete, if all are not systematically taken, 
carefully judged and presented in an orderly manner. (For example 
planet denoting 10th house matter may give promotion to the native 
but his son may be seriously ill and father pass away.) 


176 


Finally one should consider the sub. What is it? It is one of the 
nine sub divisions of the constellation, in the proportion in which they 
are allotted the number of years in Vimshottari Udu Dasa system. 
Each sub has its own lord. Hence the constellation, as the whole, has 
one lord who takes the first division sub in that star. Then in the cyclic 
order followed in Udu Dasa system, the other subs and sub lords are 
noted. 


Some planets are favourable to the native and some are evil. 
Therefore the subs ruled by beneficial ones will be fruitful whereas 
the subs of malefics to that horoscope and matter will be 
disadvantageous. 


The lord of the constellation has to indicate certain matters due 
to occupation and ownership. Whichever planet transits, it brings to 
light these matters. Whether they materialise and be advantageous or 
they fall through and disappoint the person is judged from the sub. 


Suppose Jupiter transits in the constellation of Mercury (lord of 2 
and 11 occupying lord of lagna constellation in 2). When Jupiter 
passes through Mercury star Mercury sub, an advertisement is made 
and many horoscopes are received; astrologer is consulted. When 
Jupiter moves in Kethu sub, for 4 days the girl does not keep normal 
health. When Jupiter moves in Venus sub (3 and 10), some boys 
come and see the girl. Hopes keep them cheerful. During the time 
When Jupiter transits in Sun sub, gold ornaments are purchased for 
the girl. Jupiter in Moon's sub, brings the negotiation nearer. During 
Mars sub period, one belonging to the same place and coming from a 
stranger's family fixes the marriage. During the time when Jupiter 
transits in Rahu sub, purchases for the wedding and arrangements 
go on. When it transits in the sub of Jupiter which was in Gemini in 
11, marriage is celebrated. When Saturn sub is entered by Jupiter, 
the boy takes leave from the girl, goes alone back, saying that he will 
find some accommodation and take her. Thus all the matters refer to 
her marriage. But each sub 


177 


shows the different stage in the matter. Unless one calculates and 
notes down the periods when a planet enter into a constellation and 
then it moves from one sub to the other, detailed prediction to the day 
cannot be predicted. This is the only convincing method which can 
give clear indications offering results without missing any point. 


If one wants to satisfy himself, let him do the following test. Or if 
one would like to know when his aim will be achieved, let him carry 
out the experiments as follows. It will prove to be 100% correct. This 
will assure you that what Mr. Morarjibhai told is correct. "Horoscope 
shows only the tendency and it is not a certainty. Only Brahma can 
predict with certainty" is said by some. | disagree. Mr.Morarjibhai says 
that he believes that the law of Karma governs the universe and 
therefore he believes in astrology. If a horoscope only shows a 
tendency and if one can change the event by one's will, then astrology 
has no place. If one predicts that a person will not die at a particular 
age, but he uses his will and tries to commit suicide and die, he can 
never die. | had already published that a fellow tried to commit suicide 
by hanging, drowning, over drugging, shooting etc., But he could not 
die. He did not die then. 


Everybody has the desire and will to live for some more years. 
He exercises his will, he prays, puts on Gems etc., he dies at the 
moment correctly fixed by the astrologer according to the horoscope 
erected for the moment of birth which completely depends on his 
previous Karma. Just like one has no choice about his birth time, 
place and his parents, so also there is nothing left in one's hands. 
Everything is predestined. None can change it. Then only one can 
predict using this science. 


A few examples are given to prove that correct 
predictions offered cannot be changed. 


178 


(1) | predicted to a teacher in Poona that he would have a child 
born after 5 months. On the day of query, his wife was admitted in the 
hospital for profuse bleeding in the 4th month of the pregnancy. 
Doctors declared that she would abort. They also assisted. Yet, in a 
few days bleeding stopped. She gave birth to a child and all are alive. 


(2) One brilliant student who had been scoring very good marks 
and coming out successful with distinction was predicted that he could 
not have a pass that year. Actually, he did not sit for the the last 3 
papers, as he fell ill and he had to sit for another examination next 
year. 


(3) A boy related to one of the Directors of Simpson group was 
predicted in April 1956 that he should be an engineer from 1961 and 
since he must study for at least 5 years to complete the course, he 
must be admitted in the Engineering College. In July 1956 he had no 
admission. He joined Arts College. But in September, a vacancy fell 
and only this boy was selected, and admitted. He passed in 1961 and 
from then he is an Engineer. 


(4) Please refer the letter written by a high official at Delhi, Dr. 
Tiware, in May issue. How his son was admitted in medical college 
after November, 1969 as predicted by me though college was opened 
in July and admission was over by July. 


(5) If one follows stellar astrology, with sub and Krishnamurti 
Ayanamsa, one can predict when one can have his desires fulfilled; A 
girl was running Mars Dasa Kethu Bhukthi. A bridegroom and his 
parents wrote that they would see the bride (for marriage purposes) 
on a Sunday when the star was Uthrapathrapada. | told them that they 
might not keep up the word and your daughter would be seen only on 
Aswini star day. The bridegroom was a A.RW.I. On Saturday he had 
an interview for promotion at Bombay. The interview went on and in 
the meanwhile the train steamed off. Later he was able to arrive at 


179 


Delhi only on Aswini star day and see her as _ predicted. 
She_ is the daughter of Dy.Director of Estates. 


(6) Mr.K.Ganapathy predicted to the father of a young boy who 
had made all arrangements to send his son to Germany, that the boy 
would be hospitalised for months and could not go as per programme. 
The father did not tell anybody. But actually, the boy vomited blood in 
the next week. He was admitted in the hospital and was treated for 3 
months.That year he did not go. He went only on the date predicted 
by Mr. K. Ganapathy. The boy is now in Madras. He is the Sound 
Engineer in a Cinema studio. 


(7) A person wanted to give his daughter to an I.A.S. officer. 
He had no mind to give to an air pilot officer. In spite of 
his will, efforts and determination, his daughter married only 
the air pilot officer who is now in Delhi. 


(8) A sub officer in All India Radio was predicted that he would 
leave this and take up Air-Piloting. He is now at Hyderabad as 
Air Pilot Officer. An advocate was informed that he would give it up 
and take up Cinema line. A professor was told that he would leave the 
job and take up business - of supplying fish. It had come true. Thus 
one can find that our life is like the taxi-driver who does not know 
where he will be driving his car that day. He has to drive as the 
passenger at the back seat dictates. The route he has to take, the 
number of minutes he has to halt etc., are entirely left to the 
passenger who is the God-governing Destiny. Volumes can be written 
to prove that none can dodge the fate. It has its own course. As this is 
true, astrologers who can predict with confidence, say exactly, 
precisely and correctly without mentioning "Tendency, likely, 50-50. 
and so on." 


Traditional astrologer did predict as follows. A child is born or a 
company is started or a ministry is formed. Cast a horoscope and 
predict - what? The child will die. Or the company will be closed or the 
ministry will come to an end. Everybody knows 


180 


that when a child is born, it should die one day or other. If a ministry is 
formed, it also will end one day or other in a year or two or twenty and 
so on. Therefore people want when such an end will come. If one 
says “The combinations indicate change of leadership due to much of 
confusion and the health of the political leader. Is it a prediction? 
When will the health fail? In 1967 or 1968 or 1969 or when? When will 
there be the internal trouble? When will the change come? This alone 
is wanted. This is to be predicted by the astrologer. Even a layman 
will say that this will not last long. Some may fall ill. they may 
disagree, they may fight with each other, etc., Is it a prediction? It will 
be useful if such astrologers give the month when such will happen. If 
he predicts correctly, nothing in the world can change the result. 


Useful Guide 


Coming to the point, suppose one just enters into Venus Dasa. It 
is to run for 20 years. In which sub period one can have larger profits; 
in which sub period, the business will be dull; will there be a partition 
etc., then let him strictly observe and record the results as is advised 
below : 


(1) Venus Dasa is on: Its stars are Bharani, Poorvaphalguni and 
Poorvashada. Note from the Ephemeris when Moon enters into 
Bharani, Poorvaphalguni and Poorvashada. Note the time when it 
enters, when it leaves and the date. Note down for 6 months. There 
will only 19 occasions. 


(2) Divide the duration of each star and note when the first sub 
belonging to Venus ends i.e., 20/120 of the total duration [Suppose, a 
child is born this time, we will say that the child will have Sukra Dasa, 
Sukra Bhukthi at the time of birth ]. The moment 20/120 of the total 
duration is over then Sun's sub will be on for an interval of 6/120 of 
the total duration then Moon's sub will follow for 10/120 of the 
duration; Mars sub will be for 7/120 of the duration and so on. 


181 


Some subs will pass away while asleep; ignore. But consider 
such subs on another day when you are busy. In these 19 occasions, 
at least on 6 occasions the subs will be during day time.

Poorvaphalguni and Poorvashada, you note good sales and profit, 
you will note that invariably it is ever good. It means in Venus Dasa, 
the sub period of that planet is profitable.

Dasa, the Bhukthi of this sub lord will not be helpful, in its Bhukthi, the 
business will be dull. Thus against each sub, note the results. 


They will ever prove to be true. This has been tested and found

Research Institute in between 1951 and 1961,

A LUCKY TIME FOR PLAYING CARD 


Practical Method: 


Whether one has got a horoscope or not, there should be a 
method by which it should be possible to find out the auspicious 
period in a day when one can relax himself by going to a club to play 
cards or any other game. What he should do and furnish to the 
Astrologer is a diary wherein he should note down his gains and loss 
on various days, in the past mentioning which minute to which minute 
he won and which minute to which minute he lost. Also he should 
mention the ultimate gain or sucess on that day. Then the Astrologer 
is to adopt the following principles. 


1. Take date by date. Then note down when Moon entered 
in to a constellation on that day and the duration of its stay,which is 
the interval between Moon's entry into a star and its passing on to the 
next star i.e.completing and just leaving this star. 


2. Find out the number of hours and minutes which Moon 
takes to pass on in that constellation.Each constellation is of 13 
degrees 20 minutes and to pass it, it may take 24 hours or 25 or even 
26 hours or in 22 hours it may pass; most irregular: never uniform: 
ever unsteady. 


195 


3. Then divide the constellation in the proportion in which 
each planet is alloted the number of years in Vimshottari Dasa. 
Therfore, if the duration is for X hours , then Venus takes 20/120 into 
X hours.Sun takes 6/120 multiplied by X hours, Moon 10/ 120 of X 
hours, Mars 7/120 into the total of X hours, Jupiter 16/ 120 of the X 
hours and so on. 


4. If Moon takes, say, 24 hours to pass through a particular 
constellation, then divide it by 120. The number of minutes obtained 
will be to a planet, if it is alloted one year. So multiply these minutes 
(total duration divided by 120) by the number indicating the number of 
years alloted to a planet. For example, if venus is alloted 20 years, 
then the duration is divided by 120 and multiplied by number of years 
alloted to venus. 


Since every hour is of 60 minutes and we divide the duration 
of hours by 120, it is quick to work by dividing the duration in hours by 
2. That is, if 24 hours is the duration,then 12 minutes will be the time, 
if a planet is alloted only one year, in the vimshottari dasa. When Sun 
has 6 years in Vimshottari Dasa then 6x12 minutes will be the 
duration for transiting the Sun Sub in that star by Moon. If Moon 
transits in the Sub of Rahu (as for everyyear it takes 12minutes) for 
18years, Moon will take 18x12 i.e. 216 minutes. If Mars Sub is to be 
transited by Moon, it will take 7 multiplied by 12 minutes;if it is 
Mercury, 17x12 minutes and so on. 


5. Note, to which planet the constellation belongs. Then 
the first Sub is ruled by the lord of the constellation there after in cyclic 
order, each Sub is being transited by Moon. So from the moment 
when the Moon enters into a Nakshatra, Be adding the number of 
minutes for each planet commencing from that of the constellation 
lord in cyclic order. 


6. Note down, from which minute to which minute a particular 
Sub is running in the constellation. 


196 


7. —Notedown, in which Sub period ,the native had won and 
which sub he had lost. Then one will find the group of planets which 
offer advantageous results and the group which always work against 
his interest. For the future, in the diary, note down the hours and 
minutes in a day, when lucky group's sub will be transited by Moon. 
There are the hours and minutes when one can surely gain. 


Horoscope: 


If one has got a horoscope, then note down those planets which 
are the significators of 6 and 11. Only those planets not connected with 
8 and 12 will give profit. If a planet is the significator of 8 and 12, reject 
it, as it will cause loss. Therefore, you have to erect the horoscope of 
the native. Note down the significators. Find out which are not 
connected with 8 and 12and select those which are the significators of 
6 and 11 and then work out the days when this group will conjointly be 
operating, i.e. when Moon will be transiting in the constellation of one 
of the planets, being the significator of 6 and 11 and among the sub of 
planet which is also the significator of 6 and 11 Then only, one can be 
sure of gain, even when one runs bad time . 


Accordinn to Dasa Bhukthi : 


In my experience | found that if the Dasa and Bhukthi is evil, 
then there will be obstacle for him to sit and play cards in the club 
during such short lucky time . There will be some disturbance. He will 
not be in a position to play. But the planets will create, such 
opportunity as to visit during the bad hours he will find it to be 
unlucky . Supposing one runs, according to Dasa and Bhukthi, a very 
good time, then he will not be allowed to play during the hours which 
are considered to be dis-advantageous . But he will somehow sit 
during the hours and minutes which are ruled by the benefactors and 
he will surely win. This very method of working goes to prove that 
nobody can change fate and everything happens according to the 
Divine Plan 


197 


and even in these silly matters, we have no hand in it. We are 
helpless and our fate alone, wins the race. 


Again it proves beyond doubt that Krishnamurti Ayanamsa is 
correct and if the ayanamsa is different, do not apply K.P

Only in the month of April, 1971 , while going on doing research it 
is confirmed beyond doubt that a person who wants to play Cards or 
bet in the Race Course should adopt the following method, whether 
he has a birth horoscope or not. 


Note the place of judement, provide yourselves with Table of 
Houses and Ephemeris'. Take any number which comes to your mind 
or which you see. It is preferable to have a book containing 250 pages 
and note that number on opening the book. If in that particular page, 
the page number is not printed, either you do not play or you neither 
lose nor gain. If you find any number please refer to Krishnamurti 
padhdhati Table and find out where that particular sub commences. 


Take it as the starting point .That is the Ascendant. Work out the 
other cusps for that latitude of judement. Note down the position of the 
planet at the time of judement. Find out the sub lord of the 5th cusp. 


If the sub lord of the 5th cusp is deposited in any sign in any 
constellation, but in the sub of the significator of the houses 6 and 11 
one wins most satisfactorily . Highest reward. 


If the sub lord of the 5th cusp is deposited in the sub of the 
significators 2 and 10, the gain is moderate. 


If the sub lord of the 5th cusp is deposited in the sub of the 
significator of 1 and 3 there is the gain. But it will be insignificant. 


If the sub lord of the 5th cusp is deposited in the sub of the 
significator of 12 and 5 there will be great loss and one will be 
necessitated, even to borrow money to run to bankruptcy. But if 


198 


the sub lord of the 5th cusp is deposited in the sub of the 
significator of the houses 4 and 8 one will not over trade and the 
loss will be moderate. Ifthe sub lord of 5th cusp is deposited in 
the sub of the significator of the houses 7 and 9 the loss will be 
feeble - negligible. 


This is the general rule which is proved to be very correct in 
about 95 % of the cases according to our statistics. But it is also 
found that one in twenty cases if the sub lord of the 5th cusp 
moves from one sub at the time of judgment and to the other sub 
at the time of playing the game, the results vary. Furthermore, 
research is being done and it will be announced in the magazine, 
after my next visit to Delhi where this is being done by a few 
intelligent and painstaking scholars.

First of all, one should know which method, when followed, will 
give correct time, date, month and year of one's gains in races. 


There are innumerable methods to find out the number of the 
horse which would win - when we read the books on races and 
consider the examples given by the author, the method is appealing. 
When we apply the same method on future races with limited 
knowledge we find that horse, so selected by us is either scratched or 
it chases the other horses. No other horse could be seen behind the 
one so selected: being last lost the race. 


But to a good extent the previous history of the horse and its 
present condition are considered and experienced people guess and 
they come out correct only 50-50. 


Certainty cannot be declared, as, such a sure horse tumble 
down, jockey may fall down or due to its peculiar moment in the 
course, it may be disqualified. Hence from experience one can't select 
anything as a certainty. 


Mangoes are in the tree. A boy anxious to have a fruit throws 
stones; unless he is destined to have, he cannot. But if he is fortunate 
to have, then he will have that number of mangoes as he is lucky to 
have. 

Hence, what are we to do? 

(1) Can I have gains without pains. 

(2) Is so, will it be through races. 


(3) or lottery or what. 


(4) When? Will it be only once in my lifetime or frequently. If 
frequently the exact timings. 


200 


Answer: (1) To gain without much effort one should have the 
period ruled conjointly by those planets deposited, in the 
constellation and in the sub of the significators of the houses 
(Bhavas) 2, 6 and 11. 


(2) If one is to gain through races then any of those planets 
mentioned above should be connected with 5th house. 


(3) If any of the planets deposited in the constellation and in 
the sub of the significators of houses 2, 6 and 11, is connected 
with 3rd house one gains by way of lottery. 


(4) If the significators are not posited in 2 and 12 to each 
other, luck will repeat. But if the significators are in 2 and 12to 
each other, then during the period of the planet in 12 to the other 
significator and the sub-period of the planet in 2 to the major lord, 
one will win. If the major period is governed by one in 2 to the sub- 
period lord, i.e. if the sub-period lord is in 12 to the major period 
lord, one will lose. 


But if the significators occupy any other position one will win 
often whenever their conjoined period operates. 


Yes, a period runs for months. Does it mean that in every 
race and on all days, one will win. 


No. On the whole, in this period he will have substantial profit. 
But to find out in which race and on which day. One will win, one 
has to apply only Krishnamurti Padhdhati. 


One should select the star days governed by dasa lord and 
in that the duration of the time when Moon will pass in the sub of 
the Bhukthi lord. Further to note the time when Moon will pass in 
the sub sub of the significators of 2, 6, and 11. Lagna at that time 
also must be calculated for judgement.

whole, how it could be, may be predicted by any learned astrologer. 
But they cannot offer a prediction, with scientific explanation, which 
moment one will gain, which moment one will lose and so on. 


The method to find out the moment is as follows:- 


Note down which dasa is on : which bhukthi operates. 


Then select the 3 stars belonging to dasa lord and 3 
stars owned by bhukthi lord. 


Inthese 6 star days one will gain, if both the dasa 
lord and bhukthi lord are advantageous. 


Note when - which moment - Moon enters into that star and 
which moment it leaves that star. The interim period or interval 
betwen its entry into a star and its exit is the duration of the star. It 
may be 22 hrs.or 23 hrs. or 24 or 26 hrs. and minutes. 


Take the duration, convert into minutes. Divide by 120. Then if 
the period of a planet is only one year then the result obtained by 
dividing the duration of the star by 120 will be the time taken by Moon 
to move 1 /120 of the star. If one wants to know the duration needed 
for Moon to move the Sun's sub which is allotted 6 years in 
Vimshottari Dasa, multiply the result obtained for 1 year by 6. 


If the duration needed for Moon to move the Moon's sub which is 
allotted 10years in Vimshottari Dasa, multiply the result obtained for 1 
year by 10. 


216 


For Mars and Kethu multiply by 7. 


For Jupiter by 16; for Mercury by 17; for Rahu by 
18 : for Saturn by 19 and for Venus by 20. 


When you add all these, then the total duration 
will be obtained. 


Therefore one knows, which time to which time the 
sub-period operates. 


For example, let us take this chart. He is running 
Jupiter Dasa Venus Bhukthi Jupiter Anthra. 


These planets promise him fortune or at least 
gains and success. 


Therefore note down the stars governed by Jupiter and Venus. 
They are Punarvasu, Visaka, Poorvapadrapada, ruled by Jupiter. 
Bharani, Pooraphalguni and Poorvashada governed by Venus. 


Take the almanac - Note down when Moon enters into these 
stars - on which date and at which time : also note when Moon 
leaves this star - which date and which time. (Better use K.P. 
Ephemeris.) 


This interval between the entry of Moon in a star and 
its exit is the duration of Moon's passage in that star. 


Convert the duration into rninuters. Divide it as mentioned above 
and note from which time to which time Moon was in that sub.

To take delivery of Vehicle 


Whenever you take delivery of a car, or a Scooter, or a cycle or

horoscope correctly, find out the significators of the various houses 
and assort them to know which planet signifies which house's results. 
Thereafter, one has to select such of those planets which are the 
significators of the houses 4 and 11. During the time when Moon

houses 4 and 11 and also in the sub of a planet, significator of 2,4,11 
and not the significator of 3 and 10 will prove to be favourable,

when the Ascendant transits in the sub of the 9th house, then, his 
vehicle will be damaged and has to be sent to the Garage for repair. If

significator of 12 , then there will be loss of money. If the sub transited 
by the Ascendant, or by Moon happens to be ruled by the significators 
of 3 and 12, the vehicle will be lost even by theft.

Moon in the Eighth House (cusp 7?) in different stars 


Moon is generally mentioned as a planet in Astrology. But; 
according to the definition given in the dictionary it is not; it is one of 
the luminaries. It is the nearest to the earth and it alone revolves 
round and round the earth. Daily it moves around 13 degrees in the 
Zodiac. 


It is a feminine and a changeable planet. Its effect is modified by 
the lord of the star and sub occupied by it, by the planets conjoined 
with it and also aspecting it. 


Moon is termed as "Mathi’, meaning "Mind". There are in all, 12 
planets. But astrologers have found it possible to predict the results of 
Sun, Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn, Rahu, Kethu, 
Uranus and Neptune only, the results of Pluto are still being studied 
and it is in an experimental stage. Hence, leaving Moon and Pluto the 
other ten planets may occupy any position in the horoscope. In few 
cases Moon may be in evil constellation and sub. Those who afflict 
Moon, will cause trouble through the source indicated by them: i.e, as 
Moon governs the mind, a person will be worried with such matters, 
as are indicated by the lord of the star or sub or those with whom 
Moon is conjoined or by whom Moon is aspected by. No man in the 
world has peace of mind, as Moon becomes evil by any of the above 
connections. 


Eighth house, according to Ramadayal, author of Sanketa- 
Nidhi, is the house of mystery and according to us, of misery also. It 
shows the fear and danger from enemies, danger caused by 
diseases, death, imprisonment and the like, crossing a river, theft, 
robbery, siege of a fort, fighting, wrong action etc. 


Kalidasa in Uthra Kalamrithasays that the following matters 
are to be investigated from the 8th house: 


Longevity, happiness or sorrow, defeat, bonus, money of 
the deceased, affliction of the face (as this house opposes the 


235 


second house indicating face), urinary disease (8th house shows 
bladder and genital organs), calamity, brother's enemy (as it is the 6th 
house to the third), teasing a wife's tuft of hair, enemy's fort, laziness, 
punishment from Government. (10th house shows Government 8th 
house is the 11th to the 10th; Hence, the fine paid by the native is the 
gain to the Government), fear, loss of money (only when evil planets 
occupy), giving a debt (when a benefic occupies the 8th house), 
other's money received without knowledge, permanent possession, 
wickedness, sin, murder of a living person, amputation, hearing bad 
news, chain of unlucky events, war, agony, etc. 


The kind of death, wills, legacies, gratuity, provident fund, 
insurance business, money through partner and through wife are also 
judged from the 8th house. 


It also indicates disease of the younger brother, speculative 
gains to mother, landed property and conveyance of children, 
enemy's younger brother, wife's movable property, her vision, danger 
to her life, father's life in a foreign place, or separation, honour to 
elder brother, or friends. 


To a nation, it shows the conclusion of a peace treaty, public 
morality, deficit budget, as regards exports and imports, estate duty, 
encroachment by others, loss of territory, loss of the leader, national 
grief, calamities, earthquake, volcano, floods, fire, famine, infection, 
disease, causing much mortality, serious accident, etc. 


Moon as lord of lagna in the 8th house, Aquarius, Kumba in 
Dhanishta star actually causes Raja yoga, and offers most brilliant 
results, when one in an infant stage, running Mars dasa and the sub of 
Moon. Later in every Moon sub-period he will have a rise in his career. 
Moon in Sathabisha causes worry and anxiety, unless Rahu,at the 
time of birth, is the significator of favourable houses. Moon in 
poorvabhadrapada star gives success after much struggle; it causes 
anxious ill health; it increases enemies; but one can win in election or 
competition. Partner may lose

IS CHANDRA - ASHTAMA EVER EVIL ? 


Then how has my desire been fulfilled on chandra 


ashtama day? 


The following is the letter and horoscope of Mr. H Viswanathan 
who wants the scientific explanation for his success on chandra 


ashtama day. 


On Thursday 30-9-1965 at 3.30 PM. | have been relieved on 
leave by my present employer for undergoing training in a leading 
firm after passing a professional examination. There had been delay 
in my relief to this day. Can you explain why | had my desire fulfilled 
on a chandra ashtama day? 


(Sd.) H Viswanathan 
Khandari Colony

Answer: 


On 30-9-1965, Jupiter rules the day, Mars governs the sign 
Scorpio-Vrichika where Moon was transiting and Jyeshta was the 
constellation in which Moon was passing at 3.30 PM. and the 
Ascendant was the 1 st degree in Aquarius. 


On the day you were relieved, you had been running Mars 
Dasa, Mercury Bhukti, Mars Anthra. It commenced on 19-3-1965. 


1. Generally if the Dasa lord and Bhukthi lord are strong in the 
sign, then the matter signified by them can be had when Moon 
transits in the sign, star and sub ruled by them. Actually on that day 
the star was Jyeshta, governed by Mercury in Scorpio, owned by 
Mars. Whatever be the position of Moon in the horoscope, whether it 
is chandra ashtama or not, the matter will be fulfilled. 


2. You are born in Capricorn ascendant Moon owns Cancer the 
7m house. It indicates those with whom you transact. It shows one 
with whom you work, one who is to issue orders, etc. 


Jyeshta is governed by Mercury which rules both the 6th and 9th 
house. 6th house signifies service; 9th house is negative (12) to the 
profession house 10. Hence, to get relief in service, the lords of 6 and 
9 will prove to be helpful. Further, Mercury was in the constellation 
Poorvapadra, ruled by Jupiter owning the houses 3 and 12 indicating 
changes in the routine. 


Mercury owns the houses Gemini-Mithuna and Virgo-Kanni. It is 
to be remembered that Rahu or Kethu in Gemini or Virgo will offer the 
results themselves, and during the period of the planet in its 
constellation. Kethu is in Gemini. It represents Mercury, the lord of the 
sign and Jupiter, the planet which aspects Kethu. Hence, Kethu is the 
agent of Mercury and Jupiter who indicates change in the routine, 
transfer, higher education training or probation. 


244 


Mars and Jupiter are in Kethu stars, Aswini and Moolam 
respectively. 


Mars Dasa, Mercury Bhukti, Mars Anthra shows that you should 
have a transfer. Mars, the lord of 4 in the 3 house indicates change of 
place. Mercury, the lord of 9, promises further study. 


Hence, on a Jyesta star day you are relieved. 


To your ascendant, Moon, the lord of 7, with whom you have to 
deal, was transiting in the 11th house. So the officer was helpful on 
that day and relieved you. 


Moon is the lord of 7. It was in the constellation of Venus who 
rules the 11th house counted from the 7th house. So when the lord of 
the 7th transits in a sign, eighth from the sign occupied by it in the 
horoscope, and if that lord is in the constellation of the lord of 11 
counted from the house which it owns, It shows separation. The 
period was Mars dasa, Mercury Bhukthi, Mars Anthra-Mercury was in 
Jupiter's star. Hence, on a Jupiter day, Thursday, on Jyesta star day 
ruled by Mercury, when Moon was in Scorpio owned by Mars, the 
officer had to relieve you.

degree. According to Krishnamurti System, 1st degree Aquarius is 
Dhanishta star ruled by Mars and the sub belongs to Mercury.

that area is ruled by Mars and Mercury). The general rule is that 
Chandra ashtama day is evil and that, one cannot have his desire 
fulfilled. 


The students car verify in all horoscopes and invariably they can 
find that results depend on the lord of the constellation in that sign and 
the general statement fails miserably.

CHANDRASHTAMA AND ITS TRUTH 
(moon in 8th sign from natal moon) 


Q. Why do the Astrologers advise the consultants to avoid 
Chandrashtama days ? Is there any truth ? 


We have to take only the exact position of the lagna, which alone 
indicates the person. Also we should consider the exact position of all 
the planets (constellation and sub) and offer the prediction. Otherwise 
the prediction given by either of the western or Hindu system will be 
similar to blind persons, touching any part of an elephant and 
describing how its physical features appear to be or touching some 
animal and giving elephants description. 


By Chandrashtama, Hindus mean those days whenever Moon 
transits for nearly 2 1/4 days in a sign, which is the 8th counted from 
the sign occupied by Moon in Natal Horoscope. They do not take into 
consideration whether at the moment of the birth time, Moon was in 
the very beginning or in the middle or in the end of the sign. Nor they 
take into consideration whether the transmitting planet is in the 
beginning or in the middle or in the end of the sign. They count sign by 
sign not from the exact position of Moon to the exact position of any 
planet. This is most unscientific. 


Those who are born in Ashwini or Bharani or the first quarter of 
Karthik star, will have Moon in Aries. The other 3/4 of Karthik, Rohini 
and Mrigasirisha first half, will be in Taurus : thus all the 27 stars 
contained in the 12 signs so that each sign has 2 1/4 stars. So, for 
Aries-born, Scorpio is the 8th Chandrashtama. For Taurus-born, 
Moon transiting in Sagittarius, is said to be Chandrashtama. The 
Gemini-born, Chandrashtama means that Moon is transiting in 
Capricorn. So, for Cancer, which sign transited by Moon is said to be 
Chandrashtama. Aquarius, is it not ? Thus to each sign one is to note. 


246 


Astrologers consider that planets transiting in 8th sign must 
cause damage and do harm. But the truth is not so. 


Now, coming to Chandrashtama, let me not give many 
examples to prove that no useful purpose is served by mentioning in 
the monthly predictions, the days or dates when Moon will be in 
Ashtama. Why do they give ? What do they mean by that ? Do they 
expect that Chandrashtama days must be evil ? 


They have not noted that during Chandrashtama day, some win 
the case, some come out successful in Election, some pass the 
examination, some get married, some have easy delivery of children, 
some gain by lottery, some win in the races and so on. 


is it wise, simply going on threatening people or like an ill- omen 
to mention these dates ? No doubt, whether one threatens or not, 
according to destiny one will win or gain in the period when planet 
transits in the 8 th house. 


Similarly whenever Saturn transits in many signs, the traditional 
astrologers threaten people by giving undesirable and disagreeable 
results, especially ashtama Sani, etc., They do not take into 
consideration that during Sade-Sati, i.e., 7.5 years Saturn, most of 
the people have grand success. If this is brought to their notice they 
say look at dasa, bhukthi etc., But when the twins horoscopes are 
handed over and asked why one of the twins has passed the 
examination and the other failed, they have no explanation. First of all 
they follow a wrong method. Secondly there is either limitation or only 
confusion. IF one were to follow Krishnamurti Padhdhati, he can very 
well differentiate and come out correct in all cases, by taking into 
account the "Sub". 


Let me give you an example of one of the very well-known 
politicians. The horoscope, which was published in page 20 in April, 
1986 "Astrology and Athrishta" issue, is as follows.

If according to Hindu system we take the position of Moon, it is in 
Aquarius and he has won very meritoriously with overwhelming 
majority against the wish and obstacles thrown by his opponents. Is 
he not having sade-Sati and do we not find a group of planets in 
Ashtama (in the 8th sign counted from Aquarius) ? Saturn and Rahu 
are in 2 and Saturn is said to be causing Sade-Sati. Jupiter, Kethu 
and Moon are in Ashtama. If a person takes this chart and shows to 
an Astrologer to give the results as per transit how can he give correct 
results using Hindu System ? Will not he dissuade the person ? Is it 
not doing harm to the person and also bringing ridicule to astrology. 


But follow Krishnamurti Padhdhati. On the day of the 
announcement of Election results, i.e., 9th January, 1969 in the night, 
when Jupiter, Kethu and Moon were in 8 and Sani and Rahu were in

Lords of 1,2,3,6,10 and 11th houses, whenever they, during

When Saturn transits in Pisces-Meena-and forms favourable

diametrically opposite results. If one joins a service during A dasa B

SADE-SATI AND ITS TRUTH 
(HOW TO GIVE GENERAL PREDICTION) 


Sade-Sati means that Saturn will be transiting in the 12th sign to 
the sign occupied by Moon (whatever the position of Moon be in that 
sign either in the first degree or in the last degree of the sign), then 
Saturn transits in the sign where Moon was deposited and in the 
second sign counted from that tenanted by Moon. Therefore, the three 
signs, the 12th sign to the sign where Moon was, the sign occupied by 
Moon (called Janma) and the second to it are considered to be the 
three consecutive signs which when transited by Saturn, it is said to be 
Sade Sati or 7 1/ 2 years Saturn. People who go by mere traditional 
convention and the general principles will sing a song or reproduce a 
sloka and translate it that it is 7.5 years Saturn which is harmful; one 
will be dissatified, there can be denial, disappointment, depression, 
difference, dispute, disharmony and no desirable results. Thus they 
threaten. But, | am very confident and | can boldly say that they do not 
care to correct themselves after knowing the facts. Why ? It is not 
unlikely that they know well that people born in this star, get married 
during Sade Sati, have promotion, make purchases of land, construct 
houses, possess new car and enjoy all fruits of life. Those who had no 
business at all or no profit when Saturn was transiting in the 11th 
house, (considered to be lucky according to tradition) they actually pay 
a much larger amount of Income-tax. Why ? When they have Sade- 
Sati the profit is much greater than the previous years, even though 
the rate may be reduced. 


But, when a person is to note down the stars in which certain 
people were born (the sign where Moon was) and also the 12th and 
the 2nd sign, they will find that the general Principle which had been 
expounded by our sages do not agree at all with the results which we 
enjoy. At the time when they predict adverse results, we enjoy 
pleasure. At the time when they predict and promise good future, 
there is actually much of pains. So, 


262 


generally at the time when they say that there will be pains, we enjoy; 
and we really suffer at the time when they encourage offering 
auspicious results. It is really the misfortune of the consultants that 
they approach those Astrologers who do not care to find out how far it 
has acted and how they have to modify the rules. 


Let me treat this in brief. Saturn will transit in Pisces. What are 
the three Nakshathras in that sign ? The first 3° 20' happen to be 
Poorvapadra ruled by Jupiter, who owns the houses 9 and 12 to Aries- 
borns. It is after all 3 degrees 20 minutes. Let us leave it. Then, there 
are 26 degrees 40 minutes in that sign which are covered by two 
Nakshathras (stars), Uthrapadra and Revathi. Uthrapadra is governed 
by Saturn. It owns the signs Capricorn and Aquarius which are the 
10th and 11th to the Moon sign Aries. Consider in the following 
manner. When lord of 10 and 11 transit in the constellation of lord of 
10 and 11, what will it offer ? Can anybody predict that there may be 
any evil even though it will transit in Meena-Pisces. By lordship, 
Saturn promises to do good. But if the sublord is adverse then during 
transit (Gochara) it may offer evil results. At that time, Saturn is 
incapable of doing any good; it is helpless ; its hands are tied. A father 
may have money. He would like to send Transit Money Order to his 
own son. But if there is the postal holiday, what can he do ? Similarly, 
Saturn as lord of 10 and 11 to Mesha rasi is a strong benefactor as 
regards friends, finance, fortune, pleasure, profit etc., even though it is 
Badhakasthana Athipathi which may affect only the health, if its dasa 
or bhukthi is operating or the dasa or bhukthi is ruled by the planet in 
the constellation of Saturn. Excepting that, Saturn has to necessarily 
do good to Mesha rasi-borns when it transits in Saturn's star, 
Uthrapadhrapada. So, if it transits in its own constellation it is 
auspicious and advantageous to the person if it is the significator of 
beneficial houses. Saturn gains strength. It will surely do good, 
irrespective of the fact that Pisces is the 12th sign to the sign Aries, 
where Moon was. At the time when Saturn passes on to Revathi, it 
offers the results signified by 


263 


M2).-)y the lord of Revathi. Mercury owns Gemini and Virgo which 
are the 3rd and 6th houses. Third house shows short- journey; it 
indicates that one will be helped. Also, there will be prosperity through 
the neighbours, cousins, brothers and sisters; to make money by 
editing, publishing, etc., is also indicated. As lord of 10 and 

11 transiting in the constellation of lord of 6, it shows that it is the time 
the native will take all preventive measures against infectious diseases

During election those who are born in Mesha rasi, if their lagna

Now one may ask "Sir, | am born in Libra. Does it mean that all

Consider in which constellation a planet transits. Note down the

indicated by transiting planet and the sub shows the success or 
failure, pleasure or pain.

indicate to a little extent. We are concerned

with Mars. It is in 4, in the star of node in 4. So it gives 4th

conjuntion Mars is the way how it gets strength for Kethu). There is a 
planet in 12th Bhava. Hence 12th Bhava results attributable to to

mostly, and through 12th house partly.

12. Therefore when Saturn transits at 8°14' Aries and conjoins with 
Mars, 4th and 5th houses matters will come to forefront through the

SATURN-RAHU CONJUNCTION 


Saturn a natural malefic, conjoins with the ascending node, 
Rahu, another malefic at 11-30A.M. on 25-4-1968 at 24°35‘ in Pisces 
in Jupiter's sign, Mercury star and Rahu sub. 


According to Krishnamurti Padhdhati, wnomsoever have Mercury 
and Rahu favourably deposited in their horoscopes will be benefitted 
then, whereas those who have Mercury and Rahu ill - posited must 
suffer at that time (suffering will be ill - health, financial loss, enmity, 
failure in attempts, etc., depending on the matters signified by 
Mercury and Rahu in their horoscopes). Mercury indicates the nature 
of the result whereas Rahu shows whether in that matter the native 
will gain or loose, proceed or meet with obstacles, maintain health or 
suffer etc., 


Mercury governs the signs Gemini and Virgo. Mercury rules the 
star Ashlesha, Jyesta and Revathi. Planets including nodes posited in 
any of the three above stars will offer the results indicated by Mercury. 
Mercury can occupy any Bhava. Then, the planets and the nodes in 
those 3 stars will offer the results of the matter signified by that Bhava. 


Therefore, people born in Aries, could have had Mercury in any 
of the twelve Bhavas, Though the lagna remains the same, Mercury 
by occupation indicates any of the 12 houses and so, 


273 


the planets or the nodes in any of the three constellations of Mercury, 
will offer absolutely different results. If there were to be no planet, in 
any of the 3 constellations of Mercury, Mercury will offer the results of 
the house occupied by it. As it can be in any of the 12 houses, and 
each house has its own results which vary widely from each other and 
in some cases they are diametrically opposite. Therefore, to all Aries 
borns, planets in Mercury star will not yield the same results. They 
depend on the Bhava occupied by Mercury. 


The source of the results given by the planets either deposited or 
transiting the constellation otherwise called nakshathra, asterism or 
star will be through the relations or matters indicated by the house 
occupied by Mercury and also owned by Mercury. 


Again, people are born in different signs as lagna and different 
cusps of the houses therefore, will be in Gemini and Virgo owned by 
Mercury, so to different lagna borns, the source of the results will vary 
as Mercury will be the lord of different houses. 


Anyhow, the results we have, can be broadly classified into two : 
i.e. good or bad, desirable or undesirable; successful or failure ; 
pleasant or painful etc. Hence Mercury, ruling the star Revathi, 
wherethe conjunction of Saturn and Rahu is to happen, indicates 
different nature of results to the people and Rahu sub shows whether 
it is advantageous or not, depending on the lord of the constellation in 
which Rahu was posited. Rahu might be in the 11th house but it could 
be in the constellation of the planet occupying 12th house ; thereby 
Rahu is incapable of giving a profit, whereas loss will be the 
experience. Also Rahu may be in 8th Bhava ; traditional astrologers 
may call Rahu as a malefic, but Rahu may be in the constellation of 
the planet in 11, when Rahu brings fortune. Hence, one should not 
pass a judgement whether a planet is a benefic or a malefic, only from 
the house occupied by it, but one is to note where the lord of the 
constellation is deposited. No doubt, Rahu in an evil sign can do such 
a harm as is threatened by that house, only when there 


274 


is no planet in its constellation, Arudhra, Swathi or Sathabisha. 
Therefore, if at all, any astrologer is to predict the results to be 
enjoyed by Aries-borns or Taurus-borns etc., it is only general, it is 
not applicable to all, percentage of failure will be greater than the 
percentage of success. As you want the general prediction, | give 
below. 


Saturn's transits. It is lord of 10 and 11 to Aries-borns, 9 and 10 
to Taurus-borns : 8 and 9 to Gemini-borns; 7 and 8 to Cancer- borns 
and so on. 


As the owner of these houses, it can offer the nature of results 
indicated by Mercury as lord of 3 and 6 to Aries-borns; 2 and 5 to 
Taurus-borns; 1 and 4 to Gemini-borns : 3 and 12 to Cancer- borns 
and so on. 


(1) Aries-borns : Short travel : new friends helpful ; proceeding 
on camp : receipt of traveling allowance, good relationship with 
brothers and uncle, release of books and journals, new agency, 
commission taken ; enters as a liaison officer, gets telephone, if it was

results is to be judged. This rule applies to all lagna borns. The results

Sun sign) date of birth.

TRANSIT OF SATURN 
Let us take the year '1964' 


On 26th January, Saturn passed on to Nirayana Aquarius and up 
to 23rd March, it moves in direct motion in the Nakshathra Dhanishta; 
then it transits in the same direction in Sathabhisha star ruled by Rahu 
the Ascending node till 15thJune 1964. On 15-6-1964 it commences 
to retrograde, and till 17th September 1964, it is in Sathabhisha, from 
18th September Saturn by retrograde motion, transits in Dhanishta, 
governed by Mars in the same sign Aquarius - Kumbha. On 2nd 
November it takes a direct course, and throughout the year, it is in 
Dhanishta. 


One should note that Saturn is not the only Planet which 
indicates what a person can expect in 1964. Every planet contributes 
to certain events in one’s life according to (Hindu) System (1) its 
lordship, (2) the position which it occupied at the time of one’s birth, 
(3) the houses owned by the planet (4) the house transited by the 
planet, (5) (Western System) the various aspects it forms with the 
other planets with its radical position, with the Ascendant, with the 
meridian and also its conjunction with the planets in the sign in which 
it transits and (6) (K.P) the nakshathra or star in which the planet 
transits, and the house signified by the lord of the nakshathra and 
Sub. All the points are very important, according to the system 
followed. But one should follow only K.P.System. Both the nature of 
event and the time of event will be correct only when properly judged, 
using K.P System. 


Western : - It may be said that good aspect from planet to any 
other planet may get cancelled by the adverse aspect to another 
planet. It is not correct. Both the results will be enjoyed and 
experienced. If Saturn as lord of 11 forms good aspect with Sun, lord 
of 5, and bad aspect with Venus, lord of 2 and 7, latter adverse aspect 
will not rob away the former beneficial aspect. Actually, due to the 
favourable aspect to Sun, lord of 5, he may 


278 


gain in races, speculation, or betting, or be benefitted by children, and 
because of the adverse aspect with Venus, Saturn may indicate 
ailment to wife some worries at home and also uncommon expenses. 
The day will not pass, without all these events; one will not cancel the 
other. It may be that, whatever he gained he had to spend away. But 
he ought to have felt happy for the success in speculation of gain 
through children and miserable, due to wife's ill-health, and in his 
ledger he should make both the entries, the first is the income through 
speculation or children and also the expenses of the medical bill, etc., 
due to the adverse aspect. 


So, also if a planet forms good aspect with any other planet 
which also receives evil aspect from a third planet, then also both the 
results will be enjoyed by the person. 


Thus when an individual's chart is taken, and the transit results 
are offered; by considering all these vital points, the prediction will 
come nearly satisfactory and be to some extent helpful : but for a 
general prediction all these points cannot be included, as either lagna 
or Moon's or Sun's position alone is taken, that is the main reason 
why the transit results, given in general do not come to pass. 


What Saturn can generally do : - Saturn, forming good aspects 
with other planets, will make one _ sober, thoughtful, and 
contemplative, will be careful and attentive. There will be an increase 
in one's popularity and prosperity. Saturn will offer self- reliance. One 
will be able to re-organise, and systematise his work or business. He 
will advance, to a good extent, and find much progress due to his 
sincerity, methodical approach and tact. One can look for a position 
of trust. One may not have rapid progress, but yet success will be 
slow, steady, sure and well secured. It will be long-standing. One may 
purchase land, begin to construct a building, lease out one's building 
or land, or enter into a long-term agreement. It is also likely that one 
takes excavation work, or get employed in mines, or deal with 
plumbers, 


shoe-makers, hide-and-leather dealers, masons, gardeners, 
agriculturists, coal and fuel dealers and also be benefitted through 
aged persons. 


Saturn, forming evil aspect with planets, shows that the person 
will be unfortunate. One will be discouraged due to failures in one's 
enterprises. Even petty problems will appear to be big and difficult. 
The depression and the melancholy feelings will gain possession of 
the person. However much one guard his speech or controls his 
tongue, yet he will be mistaken, if he speaks out anything: if he keeps 
quiet, and observes silence, that too, will be mistaken. What a pity it 
is. His judgement during this period will be poor and there can be no 
intuition. Even if he tries to start a new business the impediments and 
obstacles will so much depress him that he will be inclined to give up 
his projects, and find himself helpless. Further, as Saturn represents 
elderly people, only aged persons or those born on Saturdays or in 
Makara - Capricorn, or Aquarius - Kumbha Lagna or rasi, and those 
born in Pushyam. Anuradha, or Uthirattathi star, will do harm to him. 


So you should deal carefully with old people. Also if you are living 
in any old house, not at all repaired by the owner, try to shift from 
those quarters. If you work in a mine, you should be careful. Those 
who deal in the business governed by Saturn, will incur much loss. 
Compensation for the loss of the life of a servant, is also threatened. 
You have to take all precautionary measures to avoid falls, injuries 
and accidents. Try to keep company with people who are witty, and 
who will cheer you up. Join those clubs where many younger and 
more active people assemble. Even though you cannot apply your 
mind and concentrate, you be witnessing it you will laugh, when they 
laugh, and be counting days, when the evil effects of Saturn will be 
over. You will again become active and more wise than before, when 
the evil aspect is over, and Saturn forms good aspect with a benefic. 


KP General Prediction : - To be modified to individual's chart.

15-12-64 enters into Sathabisha star. 
From 26-1-64 throughout the year, Saturn is in Aquarius. Hence, 


Sign Lord is Saturn. Between 26-1 -64 and 23-3-64 it is in Dhanishta 
star, governed by Mars. Between 23-3-64 and 18-9-64 it is in 
Sathabisha, ruled by Rahu. Later it is again in Mars' star Dhanishta 
between 18-9-64 and 15-12-64, when it enters Rahu's star, Sathabisha, 
again. The above statement furnishes the Nirayana Longitude of each 
sub, its lord and the time of transit of Saturn. 


Aries-Mesha : - This refers to people born in the Ascendant - 


Lagna Aries or with Ascendant in Ashwini, Bharani and first pada of 
Karthikai Nakshathra. 


281 


When Saturn transits between 26-1-64 and 11-2-64 from 0° 
Aquarius, (Kumbha) to 1 degree 53 minutes and 20 seconds, it will be 
in Mars' star, Dhanishta and in the sub of Mercury. Mars is lord of 1 
and 8: Mercury is the lord of 3 and 6. Hence, one may receive 
annoying and irritating letters, reminders and even notices for the 
repayment of loans; pressure from bankers, or summons from the 
court. Or one may make short journeys and incur loss; or one may 
have misunderstanding with brothers, claim for partition, and receive 
his share of property from his brother. But one may gain through 
editing and publishing and publicity. Efforts will be successful after 
getting over some difficulties or meeting with some accident or after 
hot discussion, negotiation and at last compromise. 


Saturn between 11-2-64 and 18-2-64 will be transiting between 1 
degree 53' 2" and 2° 40' governed by Mars and sub of Kethu. This is a 
time for visiting holy places, or disposing of landed properties, or 
worrying oneself with the difficulties which crop up. Separation from 
family members during this period due to travel on duty, is also 
indicated. A few business people may suffer from the troubles caused 
by their subordinate staff and labourers.

relationship with higher officials and their support will be had in this

Whenever a planet is retrograde, if it transits in evil sub lord's 
portion, the malefic results will be aggravated and if the sub-lord in 
that star is a benefic in retrograde, the advantages and advancement, 
during that time, will be less than normal. A planet in direct motion in 
a beneficial sub-lord's portion in aconstellation, gives satisfactory 
gains. When it is vakra or retrograde in the same position, gains are 
assured, but it is much less remunerative for the same extent of 
pains. Hence, Saturn, a benefic to Taurus-Rishaba, will not be

Between 27-7-64 and 21-8-64, Saturn will be retrograde in 
Jupiter's sub and it is likely that the native's son disposes of his 
vehicle or house. The person continues to face many problems and 
realises that he is not respected as much as he was, due to various 
losses. Litigation and waste of money are also shown. Bills from 
lawyers and physicians will be received at short intervals and one will 
be dejected. 


As was already mentioned for Rahu sub, when Saturn passes 
between 21-8-64 and 18-9-64 in that area, depending on Rahu's 
position at the time of birth, one will have unexpected fortune or ill- 
luck. It is necessary to note in each chart where it was. 


From 18-9-64 to 9-10-64, the person will be planning, scheming 
and making many arrangements to get over the various difficulties. A 
few may make attempts to get into a new service or to have a new 
partner. There may be the chances to appeal and one can entertain 
hopes that shortly there will be success. 


Between 9-10-64 and 25-11-64, pleasant success and 
satisfactory gains are indicated. One will win in the appeal. He may 
be reinstated. Enemies will disappear. A few may even pass away. 
The immediate troubles will end. To some extent, there will be 
financial relief and also peace. 


297 


From 25-11-64 to 15-12-64, there are indications of change of 
place, heavy expenses, necessity to have overdraft, etc., After 
15-12-64, those results which one enjoyed between 23rd March and 
12th April, will be repeated. 


The above are the results indicated by the transiting Saturn. 
Other planets will contribute what they have to offer. According to 
Dasa Bhukti system, whatever is indicated will also be enjoyed. A 
beneficial transit of a favourable planet in favourable star and sub will 
not cancel what Saturn threatens, but both will be experienced.

This refers to people born in this sign as Ascendant - Lagna in

On 26th January, Saturn passed on to Aquarius and it was in the

Between 11 -2-64 and 18-2-64, Saturn in Kethu sub could have

On 23-3-64 Saturn enters into Rahu's star. Beneficial Rahu

time is the period when Saturn is stationary. 


Fortune or misfortune is never constant. There will always be a

and unexpected expenses. There may be trouble through law

LIBRA - THULAM 


This refers to people born in Libra as Ascendant in Chithrai star
